## No-inference acceptance and lifecycle hardening — October 26, 2026

Base: `c16680717f7f8a12f68ea54c2505f9242f094983`. Starting ForeCode tree was clean on `codex/app-foundation`; `git fetch origin`, `git log -6 --oneline origin/main`, `gh pr list --state all --limit 12` and `gh issue list --limit 20` exited 0. Origin/main still matched that base; no PRs/issues were returned. Work is isolated on `codex/offline-booking-acceptance`, without an upstream push target. No history rewrite, push, PR creation, merge or deployment is authorized/performed.

The application AGENTS/runbooks/workflow and external planning AGENTS/required references were read as design context. The normative pin remains `695e68a40b5f5009c9b06b83a1181e18cd6d0223`; no application contract/suite/required-check conflict was found. Local integration records gain an explicit coordination root and a separate offline scope; these are not frozen application contracts. No planning files/code/schema/tests/fixtures/workflows/history were copied or written.

### Audit findings and resulting behavior

- Installed sbx stop help says stopped sandboxes retain state; exec help says it starts stopped VMs. The old adapter's stop → exec/export sequence is therefore not accepted as process termination. Its real agent invocation/export was removed and all real experiment operations fail closed even with `enabled=true`. The full provider-budget review remains intact; no paid admission requirement was relaxed.
- Added `acceptance:booking`, a separate typed no-inference capability with a distinct scope/review/required observation set. It has no agent/model/provider/source/prompt/shell options, never touches the agent VM, and cannot import the agent/provider transport. Default validation and bundle preparation perform no sandbox commands. Only baseline, distinct-final marker, ownership regression, and regression-with-forged-PASS-stdout source transformations are permitted; their bytes remain inert on the host.
- Trusted bundle preparation reuses the existing tooling recipe/lock and snapshot/custody helpers, binding 15 files and implementation/fixture/suite/policy identities. A deny-by-default Docker context excludes candidate source. Installed ESM package scope, Node/lock/dependency tree, controller/bridge/probe bytes, worker image and Node native loader/executable closure are bound into runtime inventory. Real image/installed identities remain null until observed; tags and recipe hashes are not substituted for installed bytes. Read-only cached-image inspection found Linux/arm64 Node 22.23.4 in the reused base; the host app uses Node 24. Guest compatibility remains a runtime gate.
- Fixed writable private worker-copy modes for Next/TypeScript while preserving read-only source. Guest control is now asynchronous, cancellable and bounded; RPC replies stream within a byte cap. Fixed worker file/credential/socket/network denial probes supplement static scanning. Worker CPU/memory/PIDs/descriptors/tmpfs/time/output limits are finite and recorded; actual VM disk/PID enforcement is explicitly required from the owner.
- Cleanup terminates only registered run controllers with PID/birth binding and exact run-labelled workers, verifies absence across grader stop/restart and confirms stopped state. Interruption/failed cleanup prevents publication and retains the shared lease. Locks are not reclaimed automatically; ownership/inode/nonce changes refuse cleanup/publication/release, preserving another operator's lock. Admission, worker/cleanup failures and bounded output identities are retained privately. These mechanisms are **implemented, not observed in sbx**.
- The offline controller exercises existing receipt publication → protected loading → verdict normalization, with exact source/runtime/implementation/suite/policy pins. Missing checks stay insufficient; regression/forged candidate stdout cannot grant verification. No entries are installed into hosted server configuration. The hosted application and credential-free CI are unchanged; no execution/upload/registration endpoint was added.

## Fixed no-inference lifecycle probes — October 26, 2026

Base: local `0b56892c7613fd25a0f887474cea3eb1ae94da0c`, continuing on `codex/offline-booking-acceptance`. The starting tree was clean. `git fetch origin`, `git log -5 --oneline origin/main`, `gh pr list --state all --limit 10`, and `gh issue list --limit 10` exited 0: origin/main remains `c166807`, with no PRs/issues returned. The previous local implementation is preserved as its own commit; no history was rewritten and no publication was performed.

The private sandbox README/readiness was reinspected. It is still the infrastructure-only report last updated at 20:17:38 UTC on October 26, with booking readiness and provider hard caps false. There is no new owner authority, installed booking runtime inventory, shared-lease agreement, or current implementation-bound isolation/resource review. The explicit provisioning and execution gates remain in force; useful local implementation continued without claiming runtime acceptance. No private tools, reports, receipts or keys were copied. External planning remains read-only at the same normative pin; no frozen application contract conflict was introduced.

### Implementation and review findings

Added eight fixed harmless lifecycle cases: normal exit, observed worker exit 7, timeout with TERM-to-KILL escalation, SIGINT, SIGTERM, output flood/cap, detached harmless descendant with a forcibly killed driver, and controlled cleanup refusal followed by recovery. The suite takes only a strict request with a suite UUID, exact image/implementation identities and eight distinct host-generated run UUIDs. It accepts no shell, source, provider, model or arbitrary command input. The worker programs are reviewed fixed fixtures, not agent or candidate source.

Booking grading and lifecycle probes now use the same worker policy module: network none, read-only source/root/bridge, UID 65534, no capabilities/escalation, one CPU, 768 MB/no extra swap, 64 PIDs, 256 descriptors and bounded tmpfs. Harmless descendants advance bounded heartbeat records and repeatedly fail to mutate a read-only export marker; marker bytes must hash identically before/after each case. Workers self-expire after 30 seconds; guest suite, host probe transport and independent host recovery budgets are 90, 95 and 60 seconds respectively. Signal delivery checks controller run/PID/birth/executable identity. The timeout driver deliberately ignores TERM to avoid racing a partially started cleanup against independent recovery after KILL.

The full acceptance controller requires complete valid lifecycle observations before any candidate capture/grading. It verifies exact request/run/runtime identities, real protected driver exits/signals, output cap, advancing heartbeats, immutable source marker, expected controlled refusal and recovery. Missing/stale/inconsistent observations block grading/publication and retain the lease. A new `--lifecycle-only` mode uses the same admission/recovery path, skips candidate capture/grading, creates no evaluator receipt/allowlist entry and identifies its scope in the run record. Raw bounded lifecycle stdout/stderr are retained privately with process/output identities. Tokens/cost/iterations remain null.

After every case the protected suite checks prior controllers/workers are absent before the next case. Host cleanup now binds exact run identity and rejects **revived controller records** after stop/restart as well as remaining/removed workers. A revived controller cannot be silently recovered and counted as proof of absence. Lease ownership is rechecked immediately after admission before starting probes; ownership loss withholds cleanup/publication rather than stopping another operator's VM. The real coding-agent path remains hard-disabled. These probes address harmless nested grader processes, not guest-sudo agent supervision or general sandbox containment.

Installed inventory and bundle allowlists include five new runtime modules (15 runtime/controller files, 20 total trusted bundle inputs). No dependency versions, package locks, hosted application source/endpoints, CI credentials policy, frozen check/receipt/loader contracts or planning pin changed. The public baseline suite remains a public baseline; lifecycle observations do not establish a coding-agent experiment, Meta contribution, architecture effect, hidden holdout or product certificate.

## Post-merge CI process-disappearance race — October 27, 2026

PR #4 merged remotely as `4268fca6e002d8d8a7f5a3cfbfe4b380e166311f`. Its branch push run 40455377638 and PR run 40455399712 succeeded, but main push [run 40455778671](https://github.com/matthiasdruhl/ForeCode/actions/runs/40455778671) failed: 265/266 tests passed and `booking-runner.test.ts`'s descendant termination assertion received `ESRCH: no such process, read` from `/proc/<pid>/stat`. This is a test-helper race after a successful existence probe; the process can exit during the subsequent proc read. The helper already accepted ENOENT at that read, but incorrectly propagated ESRCH. No production process/sandbox/acceptance failure was observed by that CI run.

Fetched origin, confirmed clean local state and PR #4's merge, then created `codex/fix-merge-ci` from current origin/main. The narrow fix accepts only ENOENT/ESRCH as disappearance during the proc read; permission/I/O/malformed/live/stopped-process observations still fail closed. Added deterministic synthetic kernel-error tests, including a stopped-process deadline and both existence/read permission failures. The new ESRCH regression failed against the old helper (19/20 passed), then passed after the fix. The existing harmless descendant/heartbeat process test remains enabled; no skip, relaxed signal/heartbeat assertion or blanket retry was introduced.

Validation: affected `npx vitest run tests/unit/process-termination.test.ts tests/unit/booking-runner.test.ts tests/unit/booking-offline.test.ts` exited 0 (**82 tests**); `npm run check` exited 0 (**273 tests**, lint, both strict typechecks and both production builds); `PLAN_REFERENCE_ROOT=/Users/matthiasdruhl/Projects/Meta-Hackathon/AgentHarness-Planning npm run test:plan` exited 0 (**128 checks**); affected formatting and `git diff --check` exited 0. Only the test helper, its regressions and this provenance/log update change. The source-bound runtime/tooling implementation, 49 frozen contracts, planning pin, dependencies/locks, workflow and hosted boundary are unchanged. The service calls only the existing `createProtectedEvaluationStore`; the loader is unchanged and remains the sole application filesystem exception. Projection requires `isTrustedEvaluation` before using receipt/check/verification facts. Parsed JSON, labels, digests, assertions, spread/cloned/serialized copies cannot authenticate results. Optional genuine run records pass the existing `validateReceiptRelationships` and recorded-mode check; no missing run, task, isolation or metric record is invented.

Public projection explicitly allowlists approved recording/run/receipt/verification/check IDs, title/scope/category, public check labels/descriptions, required/optional outcomes, selected original digests, selected run metrics and approved limitations. Every observed and required check must be safely represented or the recording becomes unavailable, including optional failures. Catalog size/metadata are bounded; duplicate references and invalid approvals fail closed. Raw scopes, bundles, full runs, paths, evidence locations, diagnostics, stdout/stderr, private tests, arbitrary receipt/check text, credentials and operator configuration never enter public view data. Original integrity identities are retained and explicitly distinguished from sanitized bytes.

Added dynamic, uncached Node `/experiments`, `src/components/recorded-verification.tsx`, workspace navigation and matching existing styles. The page independently serves the honest no-approved-recording state without intents, provider configuration or inference. Available recordings show run completion separately from scoped acceptance, required failure precedence, missing/invalid/unrun required evidence, optional checks and null versus zero metrics. No A/B pair or current intent association is manufactured. The service recreates/reloads protected evidence for every served request, so source disappearance or changed/stale bytes cannot preserve a previously trusted verdict on the next response. No new JSON API, execution/registration action, production test flag or synthetic fallback; `/api/demo` remains absent/unaltered.

Fresh tests: `tests/unit/recorded-verification.test.ts` and temporary synthetic setup in `tests/support/recorded-verification.ts` (40 additional unit cases), plus `tests/e2e/recorded-verification.spec.ts` (six desktop/mobile browser cases). Independent expected outcomes cover forged/cloned/serialized input; real protected-store projection; VERIFIED, REJECTED, insufficient/invalid/not_run results; failure with another required result missing; optional pass/failure; run/receipt/verification and stale identity mismatches; changed bundle/evidence and source disappearance after success; duplicate references/invalid public metadata; null/zero/publication approval; exact field allowlisting and private sentinels; no invented pairing/linkage/isolation/benefit; inert text, stable ordering and frozen-input non-mutation; and empty/unconfigured operation before store/filesystem access. Existing custody/tamper and source-wide execution-boundary tests remain enabled.

Browser cases cover keyboard workspace navigation, no-recording/provider-disabled/no-store operation, no new evidence API and responsive widths. Populated cases render the actual component into an isolated test document from explicitly synthetic public view data; unit rendering separately receives real protected-store synthetic projections. No production endpoint, environment injection or registration populates these states. Existing synthetic credentials and external-network-denial preload remain unchanged. New guide `docs/RECORDED-VERIFICATION.md`, plus README/FORESIGHT changes, explains the boundary and future operator handoff with placeholders only.

### Delivered behavior

- Pure `src/lib/report/certificate.ts` reuses the frozen schema, certificate/reference and receipt relationship validation, canonical serialization and existing self-digest rule. Exact reference sets, explicit UTC generation, deterministic sorting and immutable inputs are covered. Pure success is deliberately insufficient authentication.
- Server-only `src/server/artifacts/recorded-certificates.ts` composes only original `isTrustedEvaluation` results from fresh `createProtectedEvaluationStore` loads, complete genuine source runs and bounded code-owned approvals. It checks source IDs, public check completeness, exact run disclosure digest, baseline and enclosing receipt/run intervals. Empty/unsafe/missing sources produce no fabricated certificate. Rejected and insufficient outcomes remain visible; fixed application conclusions do not copy raw private artifact prose.
- The original code-owned catalog adds an empty `loadApprovedCertificates` composition. Production is **NOT_PROVISIONED** and performs no filesystem access when empty. Source bytes/evidence/pins are reloaded for every serving request; changed/disappeared evidence cannot preserve a previous trusted report/export.
- Dynamic `/certificates` plus workspace/history navigation separates historical scoped outcomes from the current modeled worksheet, which has **no linked measured experiment**. Existing `/experiments` behavior/empty state remains. The existing frozen read-only `GET /api/demo` returns only `schemaVersion`, `runs`, `certificates`, `mode`; no internal check-detail projection enters it. Public runs explicitly allowlist every frozen field and require full original-record disclosure approval; null and zero retain meaning.
- The client export control receives **only ID and digest**, retrieves fresh no-store `/api/demo`, and downloads the exact canonical frozen certificate only if the same ID/digest is still available. It blocks missing/changed/failed retrieval without downloading the old displayed success. It provides no run/approve/interpret/upload/regenerate/register action and makes no inference or execution call.
- Historical intent/scenario references stay empty and decision stays null. There is no invented run, task, matched pair, isolation evidence, fairness, architecture benefit or Meta-attribution claim. Baseline comes from the approved historical source, never HEAD. The guide documents this slice's conservative same-repoCommit and enclosing-interval composition restrictions without changing frozen definitions.

### Delivered source semantics and protection

Fresh `scripts/experiments/artifacts/offline-runs.ts` binds the real fixed-root Git commit to exact captured committed fixture blobs and the separately hashed fixture manifest before adapter execution. Missing/dirty/mismatched binding refuses before admission. The private suite source plan declares all four cases A-family before execution; per-case source context fixes candidate/evaluator/suite/policy and the deterministic domain-separated `agent:none` profile before grading. Its profile binds the existing fixed Webpack/offline tools, image/tooling/implementation and limit identities. No model, arbitrary source root, command, task, runtime testing switch or public input was added.

`offline-controller.ts` now records fresh per-case IDs/timestamps and each receipt's own grading-call interval. Runs span case capture through cleanup and publication disposition; no active elapsed metric is inferred from that interval or suite/adapter duration. All five unobserved metrics stay null. Runs retain `evidenceOrigin:none`, `presentationMode:recorded`, null seed/attestation and variant A; private case names do not become public variants or pairs.

Protected `experiment-run.json` is exact canonical frozen ExperimentRunV1, separate from private `source-context.json`, acceptance/provenance and diagnostics. Writes use fixed bounded names, canonical owned/private directories, no-follow exclusive staging, mode0400, sync and atomic exclusive link publication. Completed links require confirmed cleanup, original source/candidate/implementation freshness, authentic protected-store reload and strict run/receipt/verification relationships/time containment. Candidate/implementation freshness is rechecked after evaluator I/O. Evaluator publication now also refuses replacement of an existing `evaluation.json`. No valid rejected checks are deleted. Public frozen shapes/consumer restrictions are unchanged.

Completed mechanical attempts can have REJECTED or INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE checks. An unexpected rejected baseline fails suite acceptance while retaining it and all subsequent cases. Failed/cancelled/timed-out/blocked attempts retain unlinked source records when genuine context exists; cleanup refusal prevents trusted completion. Source-publication collision/partial failure can retain an unlinked `experiment-run-failed.json` disposition; custody failures leave private partial diagnostics without overwriting originals. Cancellation/timeout retains recovery leases. Lifecycle-only and never-attempted cases create no case runs. The runbook documents every new internal acceptance field: `source`, `profile`, `agentConfigSha256`, `casePlan`, `sourceRecords`, `publicationFailures`, `attemptDiagnostics`.

`tests/helpers/offline-acceptance.ts` shares the existing inert synthetic adapter/configuration previously local to booking-offline tests; it does not add a runtime injection seam. New `tests/unit/offline-run-records.test.ts` adds **35** focused cases. VERIFIED/REJECTED/INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE, required failure plus missing evidence, optional substitution refusal, baseline expectation failure, admission/grade errors, interruptions/cleanup, source mutation (including during publication), link-validation/partial publication, collisions/custody, deterministic profile, null telemetry, cloned authenticity loss, changed pins/bytes, full disclosure, exact schemas/canonical self-digest and private sentinels are exercised. Synthetic complete-run approval is explicit and every synthetic run says SYNTHETIC with evidenceOrigin:none. Existing tests preserve observed-zero semantics and source disappearance/custody/web boundaries.

Selection requires one acceptance UUID and an independently supplied original acceptance-byte SHA-256, original suite/per-case context pins and explicit source/freshness identities. Closed bounded parsing, private canonical roots, operator ownership/private modes, single-link/no-follow custody, fixed filenames, canonical run/context bytes and original wrapper-byte pins precede protected-store authentication. The tool uses data/custody helpers only: no producer/runner/process/sbx/provider import, Git command, candidate read, directory discovery, separate installed-inventory file read, lease, runtime/admission invocation or old-review renewal. The supplied original implementation/profile is checked against the documented fixed producer and pins, never current HEAD.

The private report accounts for fixed declared cases, attempts, observations, original source references, publication failures, entries and verdicts. It distinguishes never attempted, absent original, failed publication, unlinked failure, authenticated completion, invalid and not assessed. Missing/unsupported historical or lifecycle-only source formats cannot be backfilled. Complete source validation and suite expectation status remain separate: unexpected rejected baseline and insufficient checks survive as genuine linked completed case records. All prospective cases remain A-family with none evidence origin, recorded presentation, null attestation/seed and five null unobserved metrics. Original attempt/publication-failure diagnostic lists remain explicit even when both run-file writers failed; they are accounting data, never original-source substitutes. A private-field sentinel in a frozen run is handled by withholding full-run approval, never by redacting the original or adding a supposed secret detector. Outputs are a private review and, only after validated supplied disclosure, exact private frozen demo/certificate candidates. Publication is always **NOT_PERFORMED**, runtime admission **NOT_ASSESSED**.

New output directories are private/disjoint/exclusive; original directories/records never receive writes. Files are bounded, no-follow/exclusively staged, synced, byte/custody checked and published without overwrite. The final custody-valid `review.json` is the completion marker, written after candidate files; failed output publication leaves only private partial diagnostics. CLI help/default/argument rejection perform no protected reads; normal console output is fixed statuses/counts with no private paths, full records, diagnostics or raw exceptions. This follows PR #14's preparation/interface slice. Live GitHub inspection still found that PR OPEN/DRAFT at `36f9aef80585277d49b836e54f0f847dff99dce6`, targeting main; it was not merged. The concrete sbx methods now implement exact independent admission, bounded fixture preparation, one pinned Codex invocation, outside-worker supervision, root-owned source sealing/transfer and the unchanged independent protected grader. The outer agent VM is trusted; untrusted work runs only in a network-none UID1000 nested worker with read-only root/fixture, src-only writable mount, no capabilities/new privileges/socket/credentials, restart=no and observed resource/mount readbacks. A separate watchdog removes the entire worker PID namespace before export; boot/container/request/nonce identities and an in-process finalization marker reject substituted/plain/cloned flags. No stop→untrusted-guest-exec/export sequence was restored. Streamed bounded extraction rejects aliases, hardlinks, FIFOs, directory floods and changed non-src bytes. The supervisor compiles its exact inventoried broker source, ignoring timestamp-valid stale/poisoned bytecode.

The trusted serial Responses broker durably reserves conservative input/framing/output token and microUSD ceilings before one upstream request, restricts model/endpoint/tools/history, strips transport metadata and retains failed reservations. Opaque reasoning replay requires exact content received by this same attempt and reserves the prior entire output cap again as input. Codex retries are disabled and the compaction threshold exceeds the whole100k-token budget. This does not prove unknown account access, tokenizer/framing/tariffs or vendor billing enforcement: independent reviewed protocol/tariff/account controls and spending authority remain prerequisites. The actual provider path was NOT_RUN.

The controller requires an original prepared proposal for sbx, records exclusive synced entered-stage observations, freezes the actual sealed candidate, validates grading identities and rechecks source freshness. Sandbox/scratch cleanup and owned lease release precede publication. It reloads through the real protected store, requires authenticity, validates original run/receipt/verification relationships and time containment, and writes canonical `experiment-run.json` plus private verification/disposition. A private review does not fabricate `IsolationAttestationV1`: null attestation and `evidenceOrigin:none` preserve frozen semantics.
